# llama-stack: Agent Context

[Overview](index.md) · [Agent Context](agent-context.md) · [Extracted Data](details.md) · [Network](network.md) · [RBAC](rbac.md) · [Security](security.md) · [Dataflow](dataflow.md)

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- llama-stack is a Kubernetes component.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 8501.
- connecting to external services\: aiohttp, chromadb, httpx, llama-stack, milvus, mongodb, openai, postgres, qdrant, redis, requests, sqlalchemy, sqlite.
- runtime dependencies\: Redis.
- 69 Python package dependencies (69 required), categories\: 1 data, 1 image-processing, 1 llm-sdk, 1 numerical, 1 serialization, 1 tokenizer, 3 web-framework.
- Python k8s client with 120 API call(s).
- references components\: model-registry (import), vllm (import, provider).
